## T-VERCEL-TURBO-413 run-001 — turbo remote cache adoption: 413 artifact fix + build-env hashing (operator order "Adopt Turborepo remote cache", trace 1a19d4f70c016ff9)
Agent: main (Z.ai, zai-web session web-561901d7-498c-4c6f-aad2-00aeb4727ae6)
Task: hub Vercel builds run turbo but every build is a remote-cache MISS. First-hand build-log evidence (dpl_F4FafLaeidsf687YwEhcCz6Dvsr3, 1089s): `• Remote caching enabled` is present, yet `WARNING failed to contact remote cache: 413 Request Entity Too Large` — the hub#build cache artifact exceeds the remote-cache upload limit, so the cache never populates. Control group: 4 consecutive builds (5346d33/6a9bf50/e073e30/c0c22e8) at the IDENTICAL turbo hash 08b559a35720e2a0 all missed at 901–1112s; 3 builds at hash 5ccd7fafc19367e3 all missed. With hub inputs unchanged, the second same-hash build must replay in minutes — it cannot because the artifact upload 413s.

Work Log:
- Root cause of the 413: hub#build outputs glob `.next/**` includes `.next/standalone/**`, and the build script's `cp -r public .next/standalone/apps/hub/` embeds the ~268MB `public/` tree (plus its dependency-tree copy) INSIDE the cached outputs — a >1GB single-artifact upload that Vercel's remote cache rejects. Vercel deploys from `.next` directly and never consumes standalone (standalone exists for the self-host/Docker/Render lane).
- Fix (this commit): turbo.json @syllabai/hub#build outputs += `!.next/standalone/**` (after the existing `!.next/cache/**` negation). Standalone continues to be produced on full builds; only the cache tree excludes it. Local standalone after a cache hit: `bunx turbo run build --filter=@syllabai/hub --force`.
- Latent correctness fix, same commit: `env` declaration added to hub#build for NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL, NEXT_PUBLIC_API_V2_BASE_URL, HUB_DATA_MODE — NEXT_PUBLIC_* are inlined at build time; undeclared, a future cache hit would replay bundles/prerender baked with stale env values. CRON_SECRET intentionally left undeclared (runtime-only).
- Vercel-side forensics (PAT-scoped API, read-only; zero project settings mutated, zero deploys triggered): project syllabai-hub-v2 (prj_YhOJvdlHn1RggYyPKF8ydvvLn6Bl) = git SyllabAI/syllabai-v2@main, rootDirectory apps/hub, framework nextjs, monorepoManager=turbo auto-detected (no buildCommand override — `turbo run build` is Vercel's detection, not a setting). Deployment retention: 444 hub deployments in the API window (oldest 2026-10-26, newest 2026-11-02): 357 READY / 82 CANCELED / 5 ERROR; READY durations median 407s, p90 960s, max 1936s. Arithmetic (est.): 617GB / ~444 retained deployments ≈ 1.4GB/deployment — the 617GB/10GB is REAL retention, not the orphaned-storage accounting bug. Prune (DELETE /v13/deployments) is irreversible and filed to the operator for an explicit go.
- Vercel's own build cache ("Restored build cache from previous deployment", 1.18GB upload, 28.9s) is separate and already working — it covers .next/cache (compile), not the prerender of ~4,405 pages. The remote-cache hit is what removes the prerender.

Stage Summary:
- T-VERCEL-TURBO-413 run-001: the 413 class is the reason turbo remote cache was enabled-but-unusable; this is the smallest diff that makes the artifact uploadable. Verification receipt (next same-hash production build): build log must contain `cache hit, replaying logs` and NO `failed to contact remote cache` warning; expected duration drop from ~900–1100s toward minutes. Deployment volume (a prod deploy per push; git.deploymentEnabled main=true) and retention pruning are separate operator decisions.

Task ID: T-VERCEL-USAGE (run-002)
Agent: Z.ai main agent (zai-web session web-974db600-b478-4fa9-8c8b-5a8ef6246e45, chat 3d40f07c-e73d-4c6c-a56d-254d6acecce4, trace 1a1ac3bf8794b065)
Task: Hotfix — the run-001 ignoreCommand froze the build pipeline on both production hubs; every post-#796 deploy was auto-canceled by the ignored-build-step.

Stage Summary:
- FAILURE of record (Vercel API, first-hand): (1) #796 merge a8df668a deploys canceled 15:27 UTC as superseded by (2) the #797 push (83efe752) whose deploys were SPURIOUSLY skipped by the ignored-build-step at 15:37 — despite #797 touching apps/hub/public/kg/data (a build input); (3) manual gitSource pins of 83efe752 at 15:41 skipped identically. Production froze at fb6581d (pre-patch).
- ROOT CAUSE: ignoreCommand executes with cwd = the project Root Directory (apps/hub). From there the `apps/hub` and `packages/` pathspecs match nothing, `git diff --quiet HEAD^ HEAD -- …` is always empty and exits 0, and Vercel's contract (exit 0 = ignore) skipped every build.
- FIX of record (apps/hub/vercel.json): `git cat-file -e HEAD^ 2>/dev/null || exit 1; cd "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" && git diff --quiet HEAD^ HEAD -- apps/hub packages/ package.json bun.lock bunfig.toml turbo.json tsconfig.base.json` — re-roots the diff at the toplevel and fails OPEN (build) when HEAD^ is unavailable.
- JUSTIFICATION (direct-to-main, no PR): a frozen pipeline cannot ship anything on either account; the hotfix commit's diff (apps/hub/vercel.json) passes the fixed command so it auto-bakes on both hubs. Standing operator order: "make sure the migration to the second vercel account is 100% perfect".
- LESSON for every lane: Vercel ignoreCommand/commands in vercel.json run from the Root Directory, NOT the repo root — any pathspec must be re-rooted (rev-parse --show-toplevel) or written relative to apps/hub.
- Filing: .syllabai/tasks/T-VERCEL-USAGE.yaml (run-002) + .syllabai/receipts/T-VERCEL-USAGE/run-002.md.

## receipt(T-QVIEW-MODES-1 run-001) — the player gets two view modes: "All on one page" (default) and "One at a time" — 2026-11-07 (operator order "I want there to be 2 modes … Tell me your plan first" trace 1a1b5432d196acfd → plan presented → operator "Agree"; branch t-qview-modes-1/run-001 @ main 563f2a1d)

- THE ORDER: the operator wants the shared QuestionPlayer to offer TWO view modes — (1) ONE question on the page at a time, navigated by the question-number grid at the top and Previous/Next buttons at the bottom, and (2) today's all-questions-on-one-page scroll. The plan was presented FIRST (per "Tell me your plan first") with three decision points; the operator's "Agree" locked: default "all" on BOTH surfaces (no surprise for current readers), toggle above the question-number grid, endpoints DISABLED (a paper has ends; it does not wrap).
- THE CHANGE (1 file — question-player.tsx, zero new lib logic): the mode persists device-locally ("syllabai-player-viewmode") through a tiny external store consumed by useSyncExternalStore — server snapshot pinned "all" so SSR + first paint never mismatch hydration, same-tab writes ping listeners explicitly, other tabs follow via the `storage` event; snapshot fail-closed (anything but "single" reads "all"), try/catch tolerant. The question in view is DERIVED, never stored: `viewIdx` = activeIdx clamped into the visible list in single mode (filter changes and empty sets included), passthrough in "all" where the scroll-spy owns it — every "which question is on" consumer (grid aria-current + ring, single-card gate, pagination counter, claAnchorIdx, floating-button disclosure) reads viewIdx. Single mode renders exactly ONE card of the SAME list (same keys, same real index qi — printed paper numbers from batch A, CLA anchoring, full-screen and mark-scheme targets all unchanged); the scroll-spy early-returns in single mode; goTo swaps the card and lands at the top (reduced-motion aware) instead of anchor-scrolling; toggle choreography: entering single → top of page, returning to all → restore the question being read. Pagination footer (single only, nav aria-label="Question pagination"): ← Previous | Question {printed} of {count} | Next →, endpoints disabled, counter aria-live="polite", aria-keyshortcuts mirrored. Keyboard ←/→ steps in single mode only, never while typing, in a dialog/overlay, or on the difficulty tablist (its arrows mean tabs). The toggle is a compact pill (role="group") above the grid on both surfaces, aria-pressed, same visual language as the difficulty tabs.
- LINT DISCIPLINE: the first cut read localStorage in a mount effect and clamped via a second effect — eslint (react-hooks/set-state-in-effect) rejected both setState-in-effect bodies; the fix REPLACED them with the external store + derived state (no setState in any effect body — the store snapshot IS the state), and react-hooks/preserve-manual-memoization (React Compiler) then demanded `setActiveIdx` in goTo's useCallback deps (stable setter — listed, zero churn). One TDZ hazard caught in self-review pre-run: the scroll-spy deps array evaluated `single` before the view-mode block declared it — the whole block relocated above the effect. Byte-exact re-indent of the single-mode map gate (66 article lines +2, asserted scripted — two assert failures caught a duplicated parameter line and a wrong tail shape BEFORE any write).
- VERIFICATION: tsc --noEmit clean (exit 0); eslint clean on the touched file; "all"-mode logic byte-equivalent (grid click → same setActiveIdx + anchor scroll; scroll-spy unchanged; cards unchanged — the toggle pill is the only new element in all mode); both call sites unchanged (mode is a device-local client preference, not a page decision); CI PR-gated, waited to completion before the receipt-of-record; design-behavior changes are never self-merged.
- KNOWN NUANCE (disclosed, parked to audit batch D): in single mode, when the current question cannot anchor CLA, the floating button still opens for the nearest anchorable question (join-gated) with the aria/title disclosure naming both numbers — same honesty rule as all mode, now pointed at a card that may not be the one in view.
- Anti-truncation asserts: BOTH worklogs appended append-only (new = old + blank-line separator + section; byte delta == len(section) + 1 each), asserted by scripts/tqvm1-worklog-append.py before commit.

## receipt(T-AIBANK-CARDDEDUP-1 run-001) — the AI bank cards never serve the same question slot twice (card-level dedup, both bands) — 2026-11-08 (operator order "AI bank card level deduplication", trace 1a1b6fe0cc532d49)

- THE ORDER: the operator's five-word directive landed on the tutor lane fresh after the T-QVIEW-AUDIT-BCD-1 review: "AI bank card level deduplication" — the deterministic bank cards the tutor chat serves (the T-MIG-130 BandCard rendering of the T-MIG-128/129 band views) must stop listing the same question more than once.
- THE AUDIT FIRST (before any edit): the chat client's band attach is a single `patchTurn` per stream (chat.tsx) and the thread store persists one band payload per turn (threads.ts) — NO UI-side duplication path; the multiplication class is the BANK ROWS THEMSELVES: a double-run ingestion re-inserts the same `exam_paper_id` + `external_ref` as a second `questions` row (different id, same slot), a paperless card-bank insert can double the same way, and BOTH bands serve rows verbatim — the census total ("N matches"), the shown list, the band-2 header ("N questions · M marks" — a duplicated slot's marks counted TWICE) and the copy/export text all inflated with every duplicate row.
- THE LAW (a question slot serves at most once per card): slot identity = (paperId, external_ref) case/whitespace-insensitive when the ref exists — a slot is a number on a paper, not a spelling — else (paperId, normalized stem, marks, command_word) for the ref-less rows; FIRST-WINS in the SQL's own deterministic ORDER BY (the collapse never re-orders); CROSS-PAPER rows NEVER collapse (a regional variant or a second paper owning the same stem is a real bank row with its own disclosed paper ref); SIBLINGS stay untouched (the sibling line is the candidate DISCLOSURE surface — hiding a second exam_papers row would un-disclose, the opposite of the card's posture); total, the limit cut and every marks sum read AFTER the dedup (a collapse FREES a shown slot instead of eating one).
- THE CHANGE (1 code file + its test file, zero hub changes, zero contract changes — the wire shape is untouched): `apps/api/src/services/tutor/bank-bands.ts` gains the card-level dedup projection (`distinctQuestionRows` + `questionSlotKey`, module header law documented) applied in `listQuestions` (dedup BEFORE the census total, the spec-code lookups, the figures loop and the limit cut) and in `fetchPaper` (the bound-paper rows carry their paperId explicitly — the query already filtered on `exam_paper_id` — and dedup before the item projections); placement follows the stem-hygiene precedent: a SERVE-SIDE projection, ONE law for both bands, the renderer and the relay untouched (wire truth passes through unreshaped).
- THE COLLATERAL FIXTURE REPAIR (honest, disclosed): the "limit: the census (total) precedes the cut" pin's 5 rows all shared the default `q01-x` external_ref on the same paper — distinct ids, SAME slot — which the new law (correctly) collapses; the pin's own census-precedes-cut law is unchanged and its fixture now names a distinct ref per row (the old fixture was, unnoticed, a double-ingestion fixture).
- PINS (5 new in `bank-bands.test.ts`): band-1 double-ingested slot serves once with first-wins survival and a distinct-slot census; ref-less rows collapse by normalized stem+marks (case/whitespace-insensitive) while a differing-marks slot stays; cross-paper same-ref rows never collapse (both paper refs keep serving); the limit cut applies AFTER the dedup so a twin can't eat a shown slot; band-2 double-ingested slot serves once with the marks sum honest (4+6=10, was 14).

## T-MG-CH1-MARK-1 run-001 (2026-11-08) — marking coverage for a BROWSABLE paper: 4CH1/1C June 2026

- Operator order: "create/activate it for a paper that exists in our website — e.g. one chemistry 4ch1 past paper pdf." The 1MA1/1F seed family resolve-404s in production (the RECT-2 fixture never had a deployment story that could run — zero DB contact of record), so NO paper was ever covered where learners can reach. This lane covers one that is.
- THE 500 FIX OF RECORD: GET /api/v1/paper-assets/:id/regions returned 500 for EVERY paper with a question tree (live probe, 4CH1/1C January 2023, signed-in: "operator does not exist: uuid = uuid[]"). Root cause pinned empirically (local PG 17.11 + postgres.js 3.4.9, scripts/pg_in_vs_any_test.js): the service's three array templates used the parenthesized IN form — postgres.js binds a JS array as ONE uuid[] parameter, and uuid IN (uuid[]) is a runtime operator absence. Every serving module uses = any(...::uuid[]); the regions service (RECT-1) used IN and its stubbed fakeSql could never catch it — the bug has been live since the parity phase, invisible because no covered paper existed. Fixed at 3 sites (parts/points/rects queries) + a source-law test bans the idiom and requires the 3 = any sites. paper-regions 32/32; full api suite 2884 pass / 0 fail / 13 skip; typecheck 5/5.
- THE TREE (paper-anchor driver, apps/api/scripts/ch1-anchor-tree.ts): the ADR-037 official set official-4ch1-1c-2026-06 (the operator-validated verbatim archive corpus: 9 questions / 56 parts / 95 markPoints, G7 part-sums) written into the core bank as the paper-anchored tree the regions service serves — questions (exam_paper_id = the production row ef899774…, external_ref 4ch1/past-papers/2026-06/4CH1-1C#qN per the era law) → versions (VALIDATED, QP documentId) → parts (printed sub-path labels, hygienicStem prompts) → POINTS schemes (version-level, MS documentId) → mark_points with DIRECT part binding and refs q{n}{part}-{k} in MS row order. Find-or-create idempotence (re-runs converge), ONE transaction (the createSql transaction seam), pre-write gates (part sums, ref lengths, grand 110). VALIDATED posture DISCLOSED: the source corpus is operator-validated; the derivation is mechanical (no LLM).
- THE RECTS (instrument adapted from T-MG-MP-RECT-2 to the chemistry layout, scripts/ch1_extract_rects.py): QP question/part/subpart line anchors (line-TEXT classification — PyMuPDF merges label+sentence into one span), inline "(b) (i)" rows, question lines carrying inline part labels ("7\t (a)\t Anhydrous…"), orphan-part-header drop (dotless headers with subparts), page-break multi-rect (ordinal 1), footer caps from the unfiltered line set (dotted answer lines bound the writable band — the content-only cap cut 1.c's answer area, caught in overlay review). Census: G1 56/56 labels+order exact vs the bundle; G2 zero undercounts (7 collapse representations disclosed — "Any N from"/alternative-method rows the converter models as single points; marks agree on every row); G3 per-part marks exact; G4 per-question sums == QP footers == 110. QP/MS sha256-pinned, byte-verified against the committed corpus index (564,863 / 309,629 B). Overlays sandbox-checked visually (p4 labeled parts, p17 inline subpart, p20 inline question-part). Manifest fixture: apps/api/scripts/fixtures/mark-point-rects-4ch1-1c-jun2026.json (sha256 in the receipt).
- END-TO-END PROOF (local PG 17.11, full 28-migration schema, mirrored production row): tree apply 9/9/56/9/95 inserts → seed DRY-RUN gate green (95/95 refs resolve 1:1 against the tree — the deployment gate of record, first time this gate has EVER had a real tree behind it) → seed apply 95 rects → PaperRegions.regions() = {extracted: true, markable: true, groundingComplete: true, regions: 65}, 56 part regions ALL with rects + wired questionPartId, contract-exact payload (scripts/pg_regions_final_check.ts).
- ACTIVATION (dispatch-gated, the operator's fire order per the 093/133b law): .github/workflows/t-mg-ch1-mark-1-db-apply.yml mirrors the 133d guard protocol — COW branch (Neon API, branch-local password reset) → tree driver → seed dry-run gate → seed apply → postverify (9 questions / 95 points / 95 rects) → DROP + 404-verify even on failure; prod-apply needs the COW ALL GREEN. Secrets of record (NEON_API_KEY, T138_PROD_DATABASE_URL, NEON_* vars) all exist.
- ZERO hub bytes (the affordance/overlay/marking UI already serve any covered paper); zero migrations (0017 already holds mark_point_rects). Task card .syllabai/tasks/T-MG-CH1-MARK-1.yaml + receipt .syllabai/receipts/T-MG-CH1-MARK-1/run-001.json. Branch t-mg-ch1-mark-1/run-001.


## T-MG-CH1-MARK-1 run-002 (2026-11-08) — the seed transport law: batched resolution + upsert

- Run-001's activation failed 3x at the SAME step (runs 1-2 COW, run 3 prod-apply): the seed's per-ref resolution loop (95 sequential round trips) + per-row upsert loop (95 more) crossed the Neon serverless connection reaper at the ~5 min wall ("terminating connection due to administrator command" — 16:30:01 / 16:40:17 / run 3), while the tree driver's transactional ~50-query batch completed in seconds (COW-proven twice, receipts of record; the tree IS in production — /exam-papers/ef899774 returns 9 questions live).
- THE FIX: both loops set-based. Resolution = ONE query (`mp.ref = any(${distinctRefs}::text[])` grouped by ref, the exactly-one law enforced per distinct ref with zero/multi BOTH failing loudly — duplicates in the manifest (page-break multi-rect rows share a ref) deduped before the wire). Upsert = ONE `insert ... select * from unnest(9 arrays)` on conflict do update (uq_mark_point_rect_point_ordinal). ~190 round trips → 4 queries total.
- Local rehearsal on the full schema: rects cleared → dry-run gate 95/95 → apply 95 seeded → idempotent re-apply 95 (no dupes) → regions payload real (65 regions, 56 part regions all rected + wired). Typecheck 5/5.
- Header comment carries the transport law (the run-001 evidence). Zero behavior change to the exactly-one resolution law, the manifest schema, or the dry-run posture.


## T-MG-CH1-MARK-1 run-003 (2026-11-08) — the tree driver's transport fast-path

- Run 4 (prod-only, the batched seed) hung in the prod-apply step: the driver's RE-anchor path (the tree landed in prod via run 3's partial success) takes the find+update branch for every row — ~340 sequential round trips inside one transaction over the Neon serverless WS, past the ~5 min reaper wall. The batched seed never got its turn.
- THE FIX: one probe query before the transaction — if the tree of record is already complete (questions == 9 AND points == 95 for the paper), the driver SKIPS with an explicit line; otherwise the unchanged idempotent full re-anchor runs. A re-run of the activation is now ~2 round trips for the tree leg + 3 for the seed leg.
- Local proof: complete tree → "SKIP: tree already anchored (questions 9 / points 95 — complete of record)"; the full path is byte-identical to run-001's (proven twice on COW + locally). Typecheck 5/5.


## T-MG-CH1-MARK-1 run-004 (2026-11-08) — the Neon WS loop-alive law: explicit exit after the work

- Run 4's log finally isolated it: APPLIED (178 updates, the full re-anchor) printed at 17:40:23 — 21 SECONDS after start — then 21 minutes of NOTHING, then "terminating connection due to administrator command". Run 5: the SKIP fired at 18:07:15 (1 query), then 5min17s of nothing, then the same death. The scripts were NEVER slow — they FINISHED and did not exit: the createSql seam exposes no close, the Neon serverless WebSocket keeps bun's event loop alive, the process idles with an open session, and Neon's backend reaper kills it (5-21 min depending on the endpoint's idle policy); the unhandled 'error' event then crashes the process and set -e fails the job. The batched-seed theory (run-002) was a red herring — query COUNT was never the problem.
- THE FIX: process.exit(0) at the end of BOTH scripts (the tree driver + the rects seed), immediately after the work/receipt, with the evidence in the comments. Local sanity: skip fires, seed re-applies 95 idempotent, typecheck 5/5. The workflow's postverify step uses postgres.js directly (TCP, closes cleanly) — unaffected.
- The data of record was already in prod the whole time (run 4's APPLIED + run 5's seed upserts with buffered output lost): the re-dispatch is pure idempotent convergence + the green postverify.


## T-MG-CH1-MARK-1 run-005 (2026-11-08) — the dry-run path exits too

- Run 6 got the furthest yet: the tree SKIP exited cleanly (the run-004 exit works), the seed's batched resolution resolved 95/95 against PRODUCTION in one query, "DRY RUN complete" printed at 18:25:55 — then 5min37s of idle and the reaper kill. The dry-run block RETURNED from main() before reaching the tail process.exit(0) — the run-004 fix had a hole exactly on the rehearsal path.
- THE FIX: process.exit(0) inside the dry-run block (after the completion line). Every path out of the seed now exits explicitly. Local dry-run + tsc 5/5.
- Scoreboard so far: the tree leg proved green on prod (SKIP, 1 query); the seed's resolution leg proved green on prod (95/95, one query); the only unexecuted line on prod is the apply leg's single unnest statement (proven locally: fresh + idempotent) — the next dispatch runs it and the postgres.js postverify gates 9/95/95.
